````javascript
  50: class SchemaError extends Error {
  51:   constructor(message, raw) {
  52:     super(message);
  53:     this.name = 'SchemaError';
  54:     this.raw = raw;
  55:   }
  56: }
  57: 
  58: function tryParseJson(s) {
  59:   try {
  60:     return { ok: true, value: JSON.parse(s) };
  61:   } catch {
  62:     return { ok: false, value: null };
  63:   }
  64: }
  65: 
  66: function isJsonObject(value) {
  67:   return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
  68: }
  69: 
  70: // Index of the `}` that closes the `{` at `open`, ignoring braces inside JSON
  71: // string literals (and their escapes). Returns -1 when unbalanced.
  72: function matchingBraceEnd(str, open) {
  73:   let depth = 0;
  74:   let inString = false;
  75:   let escaped = false;
  76:   for (let i = open; i < str.length; i++) {
  77:     const ch = str[i];
  78:     if (inString) {
  79:       if (escaped) escaped = false;
  80:       else if (ch === '\\') escaped = true;
  81:       else if (ch === '"') inString = false;
  82:       continue;
  83:     }
  84:     if (ch === '"') inString = true;
  85:     else if (ch === '{') depth++;
  86:     else if (ch === '}' && --depth === 0) return i;
  87:   }
  88:   return -1;
  89: }
  90: 
  91: function parseStrictJson(text) {
  92:   if (!text) throw new SchemaError('Empty response', text);
  93: 
  94:   let body = text;
  95:   const codeBlockMatch = text.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```/);
  96:   if (codeBlockMatch) body = codeBlockMatch[1];
  97:   body = body.trim();
  98: 
  99:   // Common case: the body is exactly one JSON object.
 100:   const whole = tryParseJson(body);
 101:   if (whole.ok && isJsonObject(whole.value)) return whole.value;
 102: 
 103:   // Otherwise scan every balanced {...} span and keep the RICHEST object. A
 104:   // naive indexOf('{')..lastIndexOf('}') slice breaks when prose carries stray
 105:   // braces, e.g. "result for {A}: {\"overall\":20}" slices from `{A}` (#508 G2).
 106:   // Returning the FIRST parseable object is also wrong when a chatty model
 107:   // emits a stray/echoed object (or an empty `{}`) before the real score —
 108:   // that nulls a valid score without a retry (#527 H8). And a lone unbalanced
 109:   // '{' must skip, not abandon the scan, or a later valid object is missed
 110:   // (#527 H9). Picking the object with the most keys favors the score object
 111:   // (many keys) over a small echo while leaving the single-object case exact.
 112:   let best = null;
 113:   let bestKeys = -1;
 114:   for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
 115:     if (body[i] !== '{') continue;
 116:     const end = matchingBraceEnd(body, i);
 117:     if (end === -1) continue; // this '{' never balances; a later one might
 118:     const candidate = tryParseJson(body.slice(i, end + 1));
 119:     if (candidate.ok && isJsonObject(candidate.value)) {
 120:       const keys = Object.keys(candidate.value).length;
 121:       if (keys >= 1 && keys > bestKeys) {
 122:         best = candidate.value;
 123:         bestKeys = keys;
 124:       }
 125:     }
 126:     i = end; // skip past this span
 127:   }
 128:   if (best !== null) return best;
 129: 
 130:   throw new SchemaError('No JSON object found', text);
 131: }
 132: 
 133: // Call LLM and parse strict JSON. On schema failure, retry once at temperature 0.
 134: // Attempt indices are one-based across all transport and schema retries in one score.
 135: async function callAndParseJson({
 136:   prompt,
 137:   apiKey,
 138:   baseURL,
 139:   model,
 140:   temperature = 0.1,
 141:   deadline,
 142:   signal,
 143:   timeout,
 144:   callLLM = /** @type {Function} */ (defaultCallLLM),
 145:   logger = createLogger(),
 146:   now,
 147:   sleep,
 148:   // Opt-in OpenAI-compatible structured-output request field (e.g.
 149:   // { type: 'json_object' }). Forwarded to callLLM on every attempt; the strict
 150:   // JSON parse + temperature-0 retry below remains the fallback regardless.
 151:   responseFormat,
 152:   onAttempt,
 153:   onAttemptInvalid,
 154: }) {
 155:   let lastError;
 156:   let attemptIndex = 1;
 157:   for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
 158:     const t = attempt === 0 ? temperature : 0;
 159:     const reportedAttempts = [];
 160:     let result;
 161:     try {
 162:       result = await callLLM({
 163:         prompt,
 164:         apiKey,
 165:         baseURL,
 166:         model,
 167:         temperature: t,
 168:         deadline,
 169:         signal,
 170:         timeout,
 171:         now,
 172:         sleep,
 173:         responseFormat,
 174:         // Buffer provider records so a response which fails strict parsing can
 175:         // be reported as a score-schema retry rather than a transport success.
 176:         onAttempt: (record) => reportedAttempts.push(record),
 177:       });
 178:     } catch (error) {
 179:       dispatchAttempts(onAttempt, onAttemptInvalid, reportedAttempts, {
 180:         attemptIndex: () => attemptIndex++,
 181:       });
 182:       throw error;
 183:     }
 184:     let parsed;
 185:     try {
 186:       parsed = parseStrictJson(result);
 187:     } catch (e) {
 188:       lastError = e;
 189:       dispatchAttempts(onAttempt, onAttemptInvalid, reportedAttempts, {
 190:         attemptIndex: () => attemptIndex++,
 191:         scoreSchemaFailure: true,
 192:       });
 193:       if (attempt === 0) {
 194:         logger.warn('score.json_parse_retry', {
 195:           message: `[patina] score JSON parse failed (${e.message}); retrying at temperature 0`,
 196:         });
 197:       }
 198:       continue;
 199:     }
 200:     dispatchAttempts(onAttempt, onAttemptInvalid, reportedAttempts, {
 201:       attemptIndex: () => attemptIndex++,
 202:     });
 203:     return { parsed, raw: result };
 204:   }
 205:   throw lastError;
 206: }
 207: 
 208: const ATTEMPT_RETRY_REASONS = new Set([
 209:   'initial',
 210:   'transport',
 211:   'network',
 212:   'timeout',
 213:   'temperature_schema',
 214:   'score_schema_parse',
 215: ]);
 216: 
 217: function dispatchAttempts(onAttempt, onAttemptInvalid, records, { attemptIndex, scoreSchemaFailure = false }) {
 218:   // Transport owns paid-attempt evidence. A scoring parse failure without a
 219:   // transport record is not proof that a paid request occurred.
 220:   if (records.length === 0) return;
 221: 
 222:   const sources = records.map(validateAttemptRecord);
 223:   // A lower transport invocation owns one local attempt sequence. Do not
 224:   // reinterpret a malformed sequence as a new global sequence: that would
 225:   // fabricate provenance for an attempt whose local position is unknown.
 226:   if (sources.some((source, index) => !source || source.attemptIndex !== index + 1)) {
 227:     notifyInvalidAttempt(onAttemptInvalid);
 228:     return;
 229:   }
 230: 
 231:   const lastValidIndex = sources.length - 1;
 232:   for (let i = 0; i < sources.length; i++) {
 233:     const source = sources[i];
 234:     if (!source) continue;
 235:     const schemaFailure = scoreSchemaFailure && i === lastValidIndex;
 236:     const record = {
 237:       attemptIndex: attemptIndex(),
 238:       requestedModel: source.requestedModel,
 239:       // Effective identity is provider-response-derived only.
 240:       effectiveModel: source.effectiveModel,
 241:       usage: source.usage,
 242:       retryReason: schemaFailure ? 'score_schema_parse' : source.retryReason,
 243:       minimumChargeApplied: source.minimumChargeApplied,
 244:       outcome: schemaFailure ? 'error' : source.outcome,
 245:     };
 246:     try {
 247:       Promise.resolve(onAttempt?.(record)).catch(() => {});
 248:     } catch {
 249:       // Observability must never alter paid requests or score results.
 250:     }
 251:   }
 252: }
 253: 
 254: /** @param {unknown} value */
 255: function validateAttemptRecord(value) {
 256:   const fields = [
 257:     'attemptIndex',
 258:     'requestedModel',
 259:     'effectiveModel',
 260:     'usage',
 261:     'retryReason',
 262:     'minimumChargeApplied',
 263:     'outcome',
 264:   ];
 265:   try {
 266:     if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return null;
 267:     const source = /** @type {any} */ (value);
 268:     const keys = Reflect.ownKeys(source);
 269:     if (
 270:       keys.length !== fields.length
 271:       || !fields.every((field) => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(source, field))
 272:       || keys.some((key) => typeof key !== 'string' || !fields.includes(key))
 273:       || !Number.isInteger(source.attemptIndex)
 274:       || source.attemptIndex <= 0
 275:       || !(typeof source.requestedModel === 'string' || source.requestedModel === null)
 276:       || !(typeof source.effectiveModel === 'string' || source.effectiveModel === null)
 277:       || !(source.usage === null || (typeof source.usage === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.usage)))
 278:       || !ATTEMPT_RETRY_REASONS.has(source.retryReason)
 279:       || typeof source.minimumChargeApplied !== 'boolean'
 280:       || !(source.outcome === 'success' || source.outcome === 'error')
 281:     ) return null;
 282:     return source;
 283:   } catch {
 284:     return null;
 285:   }
 286: }
 287: 
 288: /** @param {Function|undefined} onAttemptInvalid */
 289: function notifyInvalidAttempt(onAttemptInvalid) {
 290:   try {
 291:     Promise.resolve(onAttemptInvalid?.()).catch(() => {});
 292:   } catch {
 293:     // Observability must never alter paid requests or score results.
 294:   }
 295: }
 296: 
 297: 
 298: /**
 299:  * Score text for AI-likeness using an LLM JSON scorer plus deterministic shadow signals.
 300:  *
 301:  * @param {object} options Scoring options.
 302:  * @param {string} options.text Text to score.
 303:  * @param {object} options.config Effective patina config.
 304:  * @param {object[]} options.patterns Loaded pattern packs, retained for scorer compatibility.
 305:  * @param {string} [options.apiKey] Provider API key.
 306:  * @param {string} [options.baseURL] Provider base URL.
 307:  * @param {string} [options.model] Model id.
 308:  * @param {number} [options.deadline] Absolute epoch-millisecond deadline.
 309:  * @param {AbortSignal} [options.signal] External cancellation signal.
 310:  * @param {number} [options.timeout] Per-attempt backend timeout in milliseconds.
 311:  * @param {Function} [options.callLLM] Injectable LLM implementation.
 312:  * @param {object} [options.logger] patina logger.
 313:  * @param {Function} [options.now] Clock returning epoch milliseconds.
 314:  * @param {Function} [options.sleep] Sleep helper for tests.
 315:  * @param {object} [options.responseFormat] Opt-in OpenAI-compatible structured-output request field forwarded to callLLM.
 316:  * @param {Function} [options.onAttempt] Safe callback for one-based paid-attempt metadata records.
 317:  * @param {Function} [options.onAttemptInvalid] Safe callback when transport evidence is malformed; receives no provider metadata.
 318:  * @returns {Promise<object>} Score payload with overall, interpretation, llmScore, and deterministicScore.
 319:  * @throws {Error} When the operation is aborted.
 320:  * @example
 321:  * const score = await scoreText({ text: 'Draft', config, patterns, callLLM: async () => '{"categories":{},"overall":20,"interpretation":"mostly human"}' });
 322:  */
 323: export async function scoreText({
 324:   text,
 325:   config,
 326:   patterns,
 327:   apiKey,
 328:   baseURL,
 329:   model,
 330:   deadline,
 331:   signal,
 332:   timeout,
 333:   callLLM = defaultCallLLM,
 334:   logger = createLogger(),
 335:   now,
 336:   sleep,
 337:   // Opt-in structured-output request field; defaults off (undefined).
 338:   responseFormat,
 339:   onAttempt,
 340:   onAttemptInvalid,
 341: }) {
 342:   const lang = config.language || 'ko';
 343:   const deterministicScore = scoreDeterministicSignals({ text, config, logger });
 344: 
 345:   // buildScoreMathCore carries the shared scoring math (weights, severity
 346:   // scale, denominators, catalog digest) but no output contract; the strict
 347:   // JSON contract below is the ONLY contract in this prompt (issue #397).
 348:   // The contract's example row and interpretation enum are derived from the
 349:   // same sources as the math core (pack frontmatter counts × effective
 350:   // severity points, SCORE_INTERPRETATION_BANDS) so an override or band
 351:   // relabel can never leave the contract contradicting the instructions.
 352:   const prompt = `You are an AI-likeness scoring engine. Score the following text for AI-writing patterns.
 353: 
 354: ## Scoring Instructions
 355: 
 356: ${buildScoreMathCore(config, lang, text, patterns)}
 357: 
 358: ## Output Format (strict JSON)
 359: 
 360: Return ONLY a JSON object in this exact format (no markdown, no explanation):
 361: 
 362: {
 363:   "categories": {
 364:     ${buildContractExampleCategoryRow(patterns, config)},
 365:     ...
 366:   },
 367:   "overall": 0.0,
 368:   "interpretation": "${SCORE_INTERPRETATION_BANDS.map((band) => band.label).join(' | ')}"
 369: }
 370: 
 371: ## Text to Score
 372: 
 373: ${text}
 374: `;
 375: 
 376:   try {
 377:     const { parsed } = await callAndParseJson({
 378:       prompt,
 379:       apiKey,
 380:       baseURL,
 381:       model,
 382:       deadline,
 383:       signal,
 384:       callLLM,
 385:       timeout,
 386:       logger,
 387:       now,
 388:       sleep,
 389:       responseFormat,
 390:       onAttempt,
 391:       onAttemptInvalid,
 392:     });
 393:     return withShadowScore(parsed, { deterministicScore, config, logger });
 394:   } catch (e) {
 395:     rethrowIfAborted(e, signal);
 396:     logger.warn('score.text_schema_failure', {
 397:       message: `[patina] scoreText schema failure after retry: ${e.message}`,
 398:     });
 399:     return {
 400:       overall: null,
 401:       llmScore: { overall: null, interpretation: null, error: 'schema-failure' },
 402:       deterministicScore,
 403:       error: 'schema-failure',
 404:       raw: e.raw,
 405:     };
 406:   }
 407: }
 408: 
 409: // Build the example category row for the scoreText strict-JSON contract.
 410: // Derived from the first loaded pack that declares a frontmatter pattern
 411: // count, so the illustrated `max` always equals the pattern_count × high
 412: // denominator the same prompt instructs the model to use — including under a
 413: // `severity-points` override. Falls back to an illustrative 6-pattern
 414: // "content" category when no pack metadata is available (mock/test paths);
 415: // in that case the prompt carries no pattern-count claims to contradict.
 416: function buildContractExampleCategoryRow(patterns, config) {
 417:   const severityPoints = resolveSeverityPoints(config);
 418:   const pack = (patterns || []).find((p) => Number.isFinite(Number(p?.frontmatter?.patterns)));
 419:   const packName = String(pack?.frontmatter?.pack || 'content');
 420:   // Category = pack name minus the language prefix (core/scoring.md §3).
 421:   const category = packName.replace(/^[a-z]{2}-/, '');
 422:   const patternCount = pack ? Number(pack.frontmatter.patterns) : 6;
 423:   const max = patternCount * severityPoints.high;
 424:   return `"${category}": {"detected": 0, "sum": 0, "max": ${max}, "score": 0.0, "weighted": 0.0}`;
 425: }
 426: 
 427: /**
 428:  * Compute deterministic stylometry/lexicon AI-likeness signals.
 429:  *
 430:  * @param {object} [options] Deterministic scoring options.
 431:  * @param {string} [options.text] Text to analyze.
 432:  * @param {object} [options.config={}] Effective config.
 433:  * @param {string} [options.repoRoot] Repository root for analyzer resources.
 434:  * @param {object} [options.logger] Optional logger for recoverable deterministic warnings.
 435:  * @param {Function} [options.analyzer] Analyzer implementation.
 436:  * @returns {object|null} Deterministic score payload, skipped payload, or null when disabled.
 437:  * @example
 438:  * const deterministic = scoreDeterministicSignals({ text: 'Draft', config });
 439:  */
 440: export function scoreDeterministicSignals({
 441:   text,
 442:   config = {},
 443:   repoRoot = getRepoRoot(),
 444:   analyzer = analyzeText,
 445:   logger = createLogger(),
 446: } = {}) {
 447:   const options = deterministicScoringOptions(config);
 448:   if (!options.enabled) return null;
 449: 
 450:   const lang = config.language || 'ko';
 451:   const enabledLanguages = config.stylometry?.languages;
 452:   if (Array.isArray(enabledLanguages) && !enabledLanguages.includes(lang)) {
 453:     return {
 454:       overall: null,
 455:       interpretation: null,
 456:       skipped: true,
 457:       skipReason: 'language-disabled',
 458:       paragraphCount: 0,
 459:       hotParagraphs: 0,
 460:       signalScore: 0,
 461:       bands: emptyDeterministicBands(),
 462:     };
 463:   }
 464: 
 465:   try {
 466:     const lexiconAllowed = isLexiconEnabledForLanguage(config, lang);
 467:     let structuralModel = null;
 468:     try {
 469:       structuralModel = loadStructuralModel(config, { lang });
 470:     } catch (err) {
 471:       logger?.warn?.('score.structural_model_load_failure', {
 472:         message: `[patina] structural model load failed; continuing without structural classifier: ${err?.message || err}`,
 473:       });
 474:     }
 475:     const result = analyzer(String(text || ''), {
 476:       lang,
 477:       repoRoot,
 478:       burstinessBands: config.stylometry?.burstiness?.bands,
 479:       mattrBands: config.stylometry?.ttr?.bands,
 480:       mattrWindow: config.stylometry?.ttr?.window,
 481:       koDiagnosticsEnabled: config.stylometry?.ko_diagnostics?.enabled !== false,
 482:       koDiagnosticBands: config.stylometry?.ko_diagnostics?.bands,
 483:       lexiconDensityThreshold: config.lexicon?.density_threshold,
 484:       structuralModel,
 485:       ...(lexiconAllowed ? {} : { lexicon: { lang, path: null, strict: [], phrases: [] } }),
 486:     });
 487:     const paragraphs = Array.isArray(result?.paragraphs) ? result.paragraphs : [];
 488:     const paragraphCount = paragraphs.length;
 489:     const hotParagraphs = paragraphs.filter((p) => p.hot).length;
 490:     const hotRatioOverall = paragraphCount > 0 ? roundScore((hotParagraphs / paragraphCount) * 100) : 0;
 491:     // Model-output leakage (#332) is near-proof-grade and lives at the document
 492:     // level, so it short-circuits the hot-ratio score into the 'heavily AI' band.
 493:     const leaked = Boolean(result?.markupLeakage?.leaked);
 494:     // Discourse tells (#334/#391) carry no document-level floor: the analyzer
 495:     // attributes them to the paragraphs that carry the tell, so they reach the
 496:     // score through the hot ratio like every other per-paragraph signal.
 497:     const discourseTells = result?.discourseTells ?? null;
 498:     const structuralClassifier = result?.structuralClassifier ?? { available: false, hot: null, score: null };
 499:     const structuralFloor =
 500:       structuralClassifier.hot === true && typeof structuralClassifier.score === 'number'
 501:         ? Math.max(STRUCTURAL_CLASSIFIER_MIN_FLOOR, roundScore(structuralClassifier.score * 100))
 502:         : 0;
 503:     // All floors apply together. Previously leakage and the structural-classifier
 504:     // floor were mutually exclusive, so a document that BOTH leaked and scored a
 505:     // high structural floor was capped at the (lower) leakage floor — a near-proof
 506:     // leakage token could LOWER the overall score. Take the max of every signal so
 507:     // a floor can only ever raise the score (#527 H5).
 508:     const overall = Math.max(
 509:       hotRatioOverall,
 510:       leaked ? LEAKAGE_SCORE_FLOOR : 0,
 511:       structuralFloor,
 512:     );
 513:     const signalScore = roundScore(summarizeSignalStrength(paragraphs, {
 514:       burstinessBands: config.stylometry?.burstiness?.bands,
 515:       mattrBands: config.stylometry?.ttr?.bands,
 516:       lexiconDensityThreshold: config.lexicon?.density_threshold,
 517:     }));
 518: 
 519:     return {
 520:       overall,
 521:       interpretation: interpretScore(overall),
 522:       skipped: Boolean(result?.skipped),
 523:       skipReason: result?.skipReason ?? null,
 524:       paragraphCount,
 525:       hotParagraphs,
 526:       signalScore,
 527:       bands: {
 528:         burstiness: countBands(paragraphs.map((p) => p.burstiness?.band)),
 529:         mattr: countBands(paragraphs.map((p) => p.mattr?.band)),
 530:         lexicon: {
 531:           hot: paragraphs.filter((p) => p.lexicon?.hot).length,
 532:           threshold: config.lexicon?.density_threshold ?? null,
 533:         },
 534:         koDiagnostics: {
 535:           hot: paragraphs.filter((p) => p.koDiagnostics?.hot).length,
 536:           thresholds: config.stylometry?.ko_diagnostics?.bands ?? null,
 537:         },
 538:         markupLeakage: {
 539:           leaked,
 540:           hits: Array.isArray(result?.markupLeakage?.hits) ? result.markupLeakage.hits.length : 0,
 541:           floor: LEAKAGE_SCORE_FLOOR,
 542:         },
 543:         discourseTells: {
 544:           hot: discourseTells?.hot ?? null,
 545:           fakeCandor: discourseTells?.fakeCandor ?? null,
 546:           thematicBreaks: discourseTells?.thematicBreaks ?? null,
 547:         },
 548:         structuralClassifier: {
 549:           available: Boolean(structuralClassifier.available),
 550:           hot: structuralClassifier.hot ?? null,
 551:           score: structuralClassifier.score ?? null,
 552:           floor: structuralClassifier.hot === true ? structuralFloor : 0,
 553:         },
 554:       },
 555:     };
 556:   } catch (err) {
 557:     return {
 558:       overall: null,
 559:       interpretation: null,
 560:       skipped: true,
 561:       skipReason: 'deterministic-failure',
 562:       paragraphCount: 0,
 563:       hotParagraphs: 0,
 564:       signalScore: 0,
 565:       bands: emptyDeterministicBands(),
 566:       error: err?.message || 'deterministic scoring failed',
 567:     };
 568:   }
 569: }
 570: 
 571: /**
 572:  * Merge an LLM score payload with deterministic shadow-score reconciliation.
 573:  *
 574:  * @param {object} parsed Parsed LLM scoring JSON.
 575:  * @param {object} [options] Reconciliation options.
 576:  * @param {object|null} [options.deterministicScore] Deterministic score payload.
 577:  * @param {object} [options.config={}] Effective config.
 578:  * @param {object} [options.logger] Logger for reconciliation warnings.
 579:  * @returns {object} Score payload preserving llmScore and deterministicScore details.
 580:  * @example
 581:  * const score = withShadowScore({ overall: 20 }, { deterministicScore: { overall: 25 } });
 582:  */
 583: export function withShadowScore(parsed, { deterministicScore, config = {}, logger } = {}) {
 584:   const llmOverall = toFiniteScore(parsed?.overall);
 585:   const llmScore = {
 586:     overall: llmOverall,
 587:     interpretation: parsed?.interpretation ?? (llmOverall === null ? null : interpretScore(llmOverall)),
 588:     categories: parsed?.categories ?? null,
 589:   };
 590:   const reconciliation = reconcileScoreOverall({
 591:     llmOverall,
 592:     deterministicScore,
 593:     config,
 594:     logger,
 595:   });
 596:   const overall = reconciliation.overall ?? llmOverall;
 597:   return {
 598:     ...parsed,
 599:     overall,
 600:     interpretation: overall === null
 601:       ? parsed?.interpretation ?? null
 602:       : interpretScore(overall),
 603:     llmScore,
 604:     deterministicScore,
 605:     ...(reconciliation.scorePreference ? { scorePreference: reconciliation.scorePreference } : {}),
 606:   };
 607: }
 608: 
 609: /**
 610:  * Reconcile LLM and deterministic overall scores according to config thresholds.
 611:  *
 612:  * @param {object} [options] Reconciliation inputs.
 613:  * @param {number|null} [options.llmOverall] LLM overall score.
 614:  * @param {object|null} [options.deterministicScore] Deterministic score payload.
 615:  * @param {object} [options.config={}] Effective config.
 616:  * @param {object} [options.logger] Logger for warnings.
 617:  * @returns {{overall: number|null, scorePreference: (object|null)}} Reconciled score and preference source.
 618:  * @example
 619:  * const result = reconcileScoreOverall({ llmOverall: 20, deterministicScore: { overall: 60 } });
 620:  */
 621: export function reconcileScoreOverall({
 622:   llmOverall,
 623:   deterministicScore,
 624:   config = {},
 625:   logger,
 626: } = {}) {
 627:   const llm = toFiniteScore(llmOverall);
 628:   const deterministic = toFiniteScore(deterministicScore?.overall);
 629:   if (llm === null) return { overall: null, scorePreference: null };
 630:   if (deterministic === null) return { overall: llm, scorePreference: null };
 631:   if (deterministicScore?.skipped) return { overall: llm, scorePreference: null };
 632: 
 633:   const threshold = deterministicScoringOptions(config).divergenceThreshold;
 634:   const delta = Math.abs(llm - deterministic);
 635:   if (delta <= threshold) return { overall: llm, scorePreference: null };
 636: 
 637:   const overall = Math.max(llm, deterministic);
 638:   const selected = overall === deterministic ? 'deterministic' : 'llm';
 639:   const scorePreference = {
 640:     reason: 'deterministic-divergence',
 641:     selected,
 642:     threshold,
 643:     llmOverall: llm,
 644:     deterministicOverall: deterministic,
 645:     overall,
 646:   };
 647:   logger?.warn?.('score.deterministic_divergence', {
 648:     message: `[patina] deterministic score diverged from LLM score (${llm} vs ${deterministic}); using pessimistic ${overall}`,
 649:     llm_overall: llm,
 650:     deterministic_overall: deterministic,
 651:     selected,
 652:   });
 653:   return { overall, scorePreference };
 654: }
 655: 
 656: /**
 657:  * Score meaning preservation between original and rewritten text.
 658:  *
 659:  * @param {object} options MPS options.
 660:  * @param {string} options.original Original text.
 661:  * @param {string} options.rewritten Rewritten text.
 662:  * @param {string} [options.apiKey] Provider API key.
 663:  * @param {string} [options.baseURL] Provider base URL.
 664:  * @param {string} [options.model] Model id.
 665:  * @param {number} [options.deadline] Absolute epoch-millisecond deadline.
 666:  * @param {AbortSignal} [options.signal] External cancellation signal.
 667:  * @param {number} [options.timeout] Per-attempt backend timeout in milliseconds.
 668:  * @param {Function} [options.callLLM] Injectable LLM implementation.
 669:  * @param {object} [options.logger] patina logger.
 670:  * @param {Function} [options.now] Clock returning epoch milliseconds.
 671:  * @param {Function} [options.sleep] Sleep helper for tests.
 672:  * @param {object} [options.responseFormat] Opt-in OpenAI-compatible structured-output request field forwarded to callLLM.
 673:  * @param {Function} [options.onAttempt] Safe callback for one-based paid-attempt metadata records.
 674:  * @param {Function} [options.onAttemptInvalid] Safe callback when transport evidence is malformed; receives no provider metadata.
 675:  * @returns {Promise<Object>} MPS result.
 676:  * @throws {Error} When the operation is aborted.
 677:  * @example
 678:  * const mps = await scoreMPS({ original: 'A', rewritten: 'A', callLLM: async () => '{"mps":100,"anchors":[]}' });
 679:  */
 680: export async function scoreMPS({
 681:   original,
 682:   rewritten,
 683:   apiKey,
 684:   baseURL,
 685:   model,
 686:   deadline,
 687:   signal,
 688:   timeout,
 689:   callLLM = defaultCallLLM,
 690:   logger = createLogger(),
 691:   now,
 692:   sleep,
 693:   // Opt-in structured-output request field; defaults off (undefined).
 694:   responseFormat,
 695:   onAttempt,
 696:   onAttemptInvalid,
 697: }) {
 698:   // Anchor definition mirrors core/scoring.md §14-16 and SKILL.md step 4.5:
 699:   // anchors are explicitly stated FACTUAL meaning units (claim/polarity/
 700:   // causation/quantifier/negation), capped at 3 per paragraph. Stylistic
 701:   // packaging (hype, intensifiers, boilerplate enthusiasm) is exactly what the
 702:   // rewrite removes, so it must never be counted as an anchor — otherwise every
 703:   // successful de-puffing scores as meaning loss (#579). The zero-anchor case
 704:   // maps the spec's "MPS = N/A, gating exempt" to 100 because this JSON
 705:   // contract has no N/A and consumers fail closed on null.
 706:   const prompt = `You are a Meaning Preservation evaluator. Compare the ORIGINAL text with the REWRITTEN text.
 707: 
 708: Extract semantic anchors from the ORIGINAL text only, then verify whether each anchor survives in the REWRITTEN text.
 709: 
 710: Anchor types:
 711: - claim: factual assertion or conclusion ("the system failed", "revenue grew 30%")
 712: - polarity: positive/negative/neutral stance of a claim
 713: - causation: cause-effect statement ("A caused B")
 714: - quantifier: number, degree, or range ("p<0.05", "about 3x", "most")
 715: - negation: negated statement ("does not", "never", "impossible")
 716: 
 717: Extraction rules:
 718: - Extract only explicitly stated meaning. Never extract implications or subtext.
 719: - Extract at most 3 anchors per paragraph of the original.
 720: - Stylistic packaging is NOT an anchor. Intensifiers, marketing hype, and boilerplate enthusiasm ("cutting-edge", "we are thrilled to announce", "revolutionize your workflow", "unlock full potential", "take X to the next level", "comprehensive", "seamlessly") carry no factual content — removing or toning them down is the rewrite's job and must never be penalized as meaning loss.
 721: - If the original contains no factual anchors at all, return "anchors": [], "pass_count": 0, "total_count": 0, "polarity_pass_count": 0, "polarity_total_count": 0, "mps": 100 (nothing meaning-bearing was at risk).
 722: 
 723: Verdict per anchor: PASS | SOFT_FAIL | HARD_FAIL
 724: - PASS: the anchor's core assertion is unambiguously recoverable from the rewritten text; rephrasing is fine.
 725: - SOFT_FAIL: present but weakened or made ambiguous.
 726: - HARD_FAIL: deleted, contradicted, or polarity inverted.
 727: - If the rewritten text fabricates new facts absent from the original (numbers, customers, outcomes, features), mark the closest related anchor HARD_FAIL. If facts were fabricated but no original anchor relates to them (including the zero-anchor case), add an anchor {"type": "claim", "content": "<the fabricated fact>", "verdict": "HARD_FAIL"} so fabrication always lowers mps — the mps-100 zero-anchor rule applies only when the rewritten text also adds no new facts.
 728: 
 729: Return ONLY a JSON object:
 730: 
 731: {
 732:   "anchors": [
 733:     {"type": "claim", "content": "...", "verdict": "PASS"}
 734:   ],
 735:   "pass_count": 0,
 736:   "total_count": 0,
 737:   "polarity_pass_count": 0,
 738:   "polarity_total_count": 0,
 739:   "mps": 0.0
 740: }
 741: 
 742: MPS formula: (pass_rate × 0.6 + polarity_preserved × 0.4) × 100
 743: If no polarity anchors: MPS = pass_rate × 100
 744: 
 745: ## Original
 746: 
 747: ${original}
 748: 
 749: ## Rewritten
 750: 
 751: ${rewritten}
 752: `;
 753: 
 754:   try {
 755:     const { parsed } = await callAndParseJson({
 756:       prompt,
 757:       apiKey,
 758:       baseURL,
 759:       model,
 760:       deadline,
 761:       signal,
 762:       timeout,
 763:       callLLM,
 764:       logger,
 765:       now,
 766:       sleep,
 767:       responseFormat,
 768:       onAttempt,
 769:       onAttemptInvalid,
 770:     });
 771:     return parsed;
 772:   } catch (e) {
 773:     rethrowIfAborted(e, signal);
 774:     logger.warn('score.mps_schema_failure', {
 775:       message: `[patina] scoreMPS schema failure after retry: ${redactErrorText(e.message)}`,
 776:     });
 777:     return { mps: null, error: 'schema-failure', raw: e.raw };
 778:   }
 779: }
 780: 
 781: /**
 782:  * Convert a numeric AI-likeness score to a human-readable band.
 783:  *
 784:  * @param {number} score AI-likeness score from 0 to 100.
 785:  * @returns {string} Interpretation band.
 786:  * @example
 787:  * const label = interpretScore(28); // mostly human
 788:  */
 789: export function interpretScore(score) {
 790:   for (const band of SCORE_INTERPRETATION_BANDS) {
 791:     if (score <= band.max) return band.label;
 792:   }
 793:   // Above the last band max (e.g. unclamped >100): still the top band.
 794:   return SCORE_INTERPRETATION_BANDS[SCORE_INTERPRETATION_BANDS.length - 1].label;
 795: }
 796: 
 797: // Length ratio is deterministic — bucket per core/scoring.md §10.4.
 798: /**
 799:  * Score rewritten length ratio on the 0-3 fidelity scale.
 800:  *
 801:  * @param {string} original Original text.
 802:  * @param {string} rewritten Rewritten text.
 803:  * @returns {number} Length-ratio points from 0 to 3.
 804:  * @example
 805:  * const points = lengthRatioPoints('abcd', 'abcde');
 806:  */
 807: export function lengthRatioPoints(original, rewritten) {
 808:   if (!original || original.length === 0) return 3;
 809:   const ratio = (rewritten.length / original.length) * 100;
 810:   if (ratio >= 70 && ratio <= 130) return 3;
 811:   if ((ratio >= 50 && ratio < 70) || (ratio > 130 && ratio <= 150)) return 2;
 812:   if ((ratio >= 30 && ratio < 50) || (ratio > 150 && ratio <= 200)) return 1;
 813:   return 0;
 814: }
 815: 
 816: /**
 817:  * Score fidelity between original and rewritten text using length plus LLM criteria.
 818:  *
 819:  * @param {object} options Fidelity options.
 820:  * @param {string} options.original Original text.
 821:  * @param {string} options.rewritten Rewritten text.
 822:  * @param {string} [options.apiKey] Provider API key.
 823:  * @param {string} [options.baseURL] Provider base URL.
 824:  * @param {string} [options.model] Model id.
 825:  * @param {number} [options.deadline] Absolute epoch-millisecond deadline.
 826:  * @param {AbortSignal} [options.signal] External cancellation signal.
 827:  * @param {number} [options.timeout] Per-attempt backend timeout in milliseconds.
 828:  * @param {Function} [options.callLLM] Injectable LLM implementation.
 829:  * @param {object} [options.logger] patina logger.
 830:  * @param {Function} [options.now] Clock returning epoch milliseconds.
 831:  * @param {Function} [options.sleep] Sleep helper for tests.
 832:  * @param {object} [options.responseFormat] Opt-in OpenAI-compatible structured-output request field forwarded to callLLM.
 833:  * @param {Function} [options.onAttempt] Safe callback for one-based paid-attempt metadata records.
 834:  * @param {Function} [options.onAttemptInvalid] Safe callback when transport evidence is malformed; receives no provider metadata.
 835:  * @returns {Promise<Object>} Fidelity result.
 836:  * @throws {Error} When the operation is aborted.
 837:  * @example
 838:  * const fidelity = await scoreFidelity({ original: 'A', rewritten: 'A', callLLM: async () => '{"criteria":{"meaning":3,"tone":3,"no_unintended_additions":3}}' });
 839:  */
 840: export async function scoreFidelity({
 841:   original,
 842:   rewritten,
 843:   apiKey,
 844:   baseURL,
 845:   model,
 846:   deadline,
 847:   signal,
 848:   timeout,
 849:   callLLM = defaultCallLLM,
 850:   logger = createLogger(),
 851:   now,
 852:   sleep,
 853:   // Opt-in structured-output request field; defaults off (undefined).
 854:   responseFormat,
 855:   onAttempt,
 856:   onAttemptInvalid,
 857: }) {
 858:   // Length is deterministic; only ask LLM for the three judgment criteria.
 859:   const lengthPoints = lengthRatioPoints(original, rewritten);
 860:   const lengthRatio = original ? Math.round((rewritten.length / original.length) * 100) : 100;
 861: 
 862:   const prompt = `You are a Fidelity evaluator. Compare ORIGINAL vs REWRITTEN text and score three criteria.
 863: 
 864: Each criterion: 0-3 points. High=3 (preserved), Medium=2 (minor drift), Low=1 (noticeable drift), Fail=0 (broken).
 865: 
 866: Criteria:
 867: 1. claims_preserved — every factual claim in ORIGINAL appears (perhaps rephrased) in REWRITTEN.
 868: 2. no_fabrication — REWRITTEN does not add claims/facts not present in ORIGINAL.
 869: 3. tone_match — register/formality of REWRITTEN matches ORIGINAL.
 870: 
 871: Return ONLY this JSON, no markdown:
 872: 
 873: {
 874:   "claims_preserved": 0,
 875:   "no_fabrication": 0,
 876:   "tone_match": 0,
 877:   "rationale": "one sentence per criterion"
 878: }
 879: 
 880: ## ORIGINAL
 881: 
 882: ${original}
 883: 
 884: ## REWRITTEN
 885: 
 886: ${rewritten}
 887: `;
 888: 
 889:   let parsed = null;
 890:   let schemaError = null;
 891:   try {
 892:     const result = await callAndParseJson({
 893:       prompt,
 894:       apiKey,
 895:       baseURL,
 896:       model,
 897:       deadline,
 898:       signal,
 899:       callLLM,
 900:       timeout,
 901:       logger,
 902:       now,
 903:       sleep,
 904:       responseFormat,
 905:       onAttempt,
 906:       onAttemptInvalid,
 907:     });
 908:     parsed = result.parsed;
 909:   } catch (e) {
 910:     rethrowIfAborted(e, signal);
 911:     logger.warn('score.fidelity_schema_failure', {
 912:       message: `[patina] scoreFidelity schema failure after retry: ${redactErrorText(e.message)}`,
 913:     });
 914:     schemaError = e;
 915:   }
 916: 
 917:   const claims = clamp03(parsed?.claims_preserved);
 918:   const noFab = clamp03(parsed?.no_fabrication);
 919:   const tone = clamp03(parsed?.tone_match);
 920:   const fidelity = ((claims + noFab + tone + lengthPoints) / 12) * 100;
 921: 
 922:   return {
 923:     criteria: {
 924:       claims_preserved: claims,
 925:       no_fabrication: noFab,
 926:       tone_match: tone,
 927:       length_ratio: lengthPoints,
 928:     },
 929:     length_ratio_pct: lengthRatio,
 930:     rationale: parsed?.rationale ?? null,
 931:     fidelity: Math.round(fidelity * 10) / 10,
 932:     ...(schemaError ? { error: 'schema-failure', raw: schemaError.raw } : {}),
 933:   };
 934: }
 935: 
 936: /**
 937:  * Clamp and round a value into the inclusive 0-3 scoring range.
 938:  *
 939:  * @param {number|string} v Value to clamp.
 940:  * @returns {number} Integer from 0 to 3.
 941:  * @example
 942:  * const value = clamp03(4.2); // 3
 943:  */
 944: export function clamp03(v) {
 945:   const n = Number(v);
 946:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
 947:   if (n < 0) return 0;
 948:   if (n > 3) return 3;
 949:   return Math.round(n);
 950: }
 951: 
 952: function rethrowIfAborted(err, signal) {
 953:   if (signal?.aborted || err?.name === 'AbortError') throw err;
 954: }
 955: 
 956: // Combined score per core/scoring.md §13: AI-likeness × ai_weight + (100 - fidelity) × fidelity_weight.
 957: // Lower is better. Falls back to default weights if profile not configured.
 958: /**
 959:  * Combine AI-likeness, inverted fidelity, and optional deterministic score.
 960:  *
 961:  * @param {object} options Combined score inputs.
 962:  * @param {number} options.aiLikeness AI-likeness score, lower is better.
 963:  * @param {number} options.fidelity Fidelity score, higher is better.
 964:  * @param {string} [options.profile] Profile name for configured weights.
 965:  * @param {object} [options.config] Effective config.
 966:  * @param {number|object|null} [options.deterministicScore] Optional deterministic score.
 967:  * @returns {number} Combined score, lower is better.
 968:  * @example
 969:  * const score = combinedScore({ aiLikeness: 20, fidelity: 90, profile: 'default', config: {} });
 970:  */
 971: export function combinedScore({ aiLikeness, fidelity, profile, config, deterministicScore }) {
 972:   const profileWeights = config?.ouroboros?.['combined-weights']?.[profile];
 973:   const ai = profileWeights?.['ai-likeness'] ?? 0.6;
 974:   const fid = profileWeights?.fidelity ?? 0.4;
 975:   const deterministicWeight = deterministicScoringOptions(config).combinedWeight;
 976:   const deterministic = toFiniteScore(deterministicScore?.overall ?? deterministicScore);
 977:   const fidelityInverted = 100 - fidelity;
 978:   if (deterministicWeight > 0 && deterministic !== null) {
 979:     const totalWeight = ai + fid + deterministicWeight;
 980:     return roundScore(
 981:       (aiLikeness * ai + fidelityInverted * fid + deterministic * deterministicWeight) /
 982:         totalWeight
 983:     );
 984:   }
 985:   return roundScore(aiLikeness * ai + fidelityInverted * fid);
 986: }
 987: 
 988: function isLexiconEnabledForLanguage(config = {}, lang) {
 989:   if (config.lexicon?.enabled === false) return false;
 990:   const enabledLanguages = config.lexicon?.languages;
 991:   return !Array.isArray(enabledLanguages) || enabledLanguages.includes(lang);
 992: }
 993: 
 994: function deterministicScoringOptions(config = {}) {
 995:   const cfg = config.scoring?.deterministic || {};
 996:   const enabled = cfg.enabled !== false;
 997:   const divergenceThreshold = Math.max(0, positiveNumber(
 998:     cfg['divergence-threshold'] ?? cfg.divergenceThreshold,
 999:     DEFAULT_DETERMINISTIC_DIVERGENCE_THRESHOLD
1000:   ));
1001:   const combinedWeight = Math.max(0, positiveNumber(
1002:     cfg['combined-weight'] ?? cfg.combinedWeight,
1003:     0
1004:   ));
1005:   return { enabled, divergenceThreshold, combinedWeight };
1006: }
1007: 
1008: function positiveNumber(value, fallback) {
1009:   const n = Number(value);
1010:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
1011: }
1012: 
1013: function countBands(values) {
1014:   const counts = { low: 0, mid: 0, high: 0, null: 0 };
1015:   for (const value of values) {
1016:     if (value === 'low' || value === 'mid' || value === 'high') counts[value]++;
1017:     else counts.null++;
1018:   }
1019:   return counts;
1020: }
1021: 
1022: function emptyDeterministicBands() {
1023:   return {
1024:     burstiness: { low: 0, mid: 0, high: 0, null: 0 },
1025:     mattr: { low: 0, mid: 0, high: 0, null: 0 },
1026:     lexicon: { hot: 0, threshold: null },
1027:     koDiagnostics: { hot: 0, thresholds: null },
1028:     markupLeakage: { leaked: false, hits: 0, floor: LEAKAGE_SCORE_FLOOR },
1029:     discourseTells: { hot: null, fakeCandor: null, thematicBreaks: null },
1030:     structuralClassifier: { available: false, hot: null, score: null, floor: 0 },
1031:   };
1032: }
1033: 
1034: function toFiniteScore(value) {
1035:   if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') return null;
1036:   const n = Number(value);
1037:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
1038: }
1039: 
1040: function roundScore(value) {
1041:   return Math.round(value * 10) / 10;
1042: }
````

## File: .patina.default.yaml
````yaml
  1: version: "6.3.1"
  2: language: ko              # Korean (default) -- auto-loads all ko-*.md patterns
  3:                           # language: en      -- English -- auto-loads all en-*.md patterns
  4:                           # language: zh      -- Chinese -- auto-loads all zh-*.md patterns
  5:                           # language: ja      -- Japanese -- auto-loads all ja-*.md patterns
  6:                           # Override with CLI: --lang en / --lang zh / --lang ja
  7: profile: default
  8: output: rewrite           # rewrite | diff | audit | score
  9: 
 10: # Opt-in: request OpenAI-compatible structured output (response_format
 11: # json_object) for ouroboros LLM scoring. Default false; only the openai-http
 12: # backend forwards it (never sent to local CLI backends). Some OpenAI-compatible
 13: # endpoints reject response_format — leave false unless your endpoint supports it.
 14: structured-output: false
 15: 
 16: # Tone categorization (v3.10): register axis (casual/professional) for ko/en.
 17: # Resolution: explicit --tone > config tone > config profile.
 18: # auto = heuristic single-tone detection at SKILL.md Phase 4.5b.
 19: # Unset = profile-only legacy behavior (regression-safe default).
 20: tone:                       # one of: casual, professional, auto (register only)
 21:                             # Override with CLI: --tone <name>
 22:                             # zh/ja with explicit tone: warns and falls back to profile.
 23: 
 24: # Persona harness (v1): Korean rewrite defaults to the conservative preserve
 25: # persona. Non-rewrite and non-Korean surfaces do not apply persona implicitly.
 26: persona: preserve
 27: personas:
 28:   library: personas/ko
 29:   thresholds:
 30:     schema: patina.persona.thresholds.v1
 31:     # source stays `placeholder` because the advisory tuning below is informed by
 32:     # observation, not a formal 2-round promotion. The ENFORCING floors, however,
 33:     # are the product's core safety contract and do not depend on calibration.
 34:     source: placeholder
 35:     calibration_rounds_required: 2
 36:     # --- ENFORCING (safety gate; a miss sets a non-zero exit, non-destructive) ---
 37:     # Meaning + facts only. Enforced when evaluated (mps/fidelity need --verify or a
 38:     # backend-reported score); dropped source numbers always enforce deterministically.
 39:     mps_floor: 70
 40:     fidelity_floor: 70
 41:     # --- ADVISORY (warn only; never blocks or changes the exit code) ---
 42:     # persona_match: voice-quality signal. Observed real KO persona-match runs ~35-56,
 43:     # so 45 flags the low end without firing on every rewrite.
 44:     persona_match_min: 45
 45:     # churn: surface token change, NOT a meaning signal. Live KO humanizing rewrites
 46:     # churn ~0.5-0.85 while preserving meaning, so 0.9 flags only near-total rewrites.
 47:     churn_max: 0.9
 48: 
 49: # Supported languages: ko, en, zh, ja
 50: 
 51: # patterns 목록은 기본 언어(ko)의 팩을 문서화합니다.
 52: # 실제 로딩은 Glob patterns/{lang}-*.md로 자동 탐색합니다.
 53: # 특정 팩을 제외하려면 skip-patterns를 사용하세요.
 54: patterns:

 87: skip-patterns: []          # additive merge across default/global/project configs
 88: blocklist: []              # additive merge: 추가로 감지할 어휘
 89: allowlist: []              # additive merge: 감지에서 제외할 어휘
 90: # Other arrays are replaced by the higher-precedence config so users can choose
 91: # an exact value instead of merging by accident.
 92: 
 93: # Over-editing guard: before a rewrite, if the deterministic layer finds
 94: # nothing to fix (0 hot paragraphs, near-zero signal, 3+ paragraphs), warn that
 95: # rewriting already-human text can ADD AI-likeness (measured on human documents
 96: # in docs/research/2026-rewrite-efficacy-study1.md). Advisory only — never
 97: # blocks. Set to false to silence.
 98: over-editing-guard: true
 99: 
100: # Scoring: LLM score remains canonical, with a deterministic shadow score
101: # from src/features/* for reproducible drift checks.
102: scoring:
103:   deterministic:
104:     enabled: true
105:     divergence-threshold: 20  # warn and prefer the more pessimistic score above this delta
106:     combined-weight: 0        # default off for combinedScore; users may opt in (e.g. 0.2)

111: ouroboros:

113:   target-score: 30          # stop when overall score <= this value (0-100)
114:   max-iterations: 3         # hard limit on loop iterations
115:   plateau-threshold: 10     # stop if score improves by less than this between iterations

187: # Stylometry: deterministic statistical preprocessing (SKILL.md Step 4.6)
188: # Calculates burstiness (sentence-length CV) and MATTR (lexical diversity) per
189: # paragraph, marks suspect zones, and feeds them into 5a/5b as internal memory.
190: # See core/stylometry.md for the full algorithm reference.
191: stylometry:
192:   enabled: true              # set false to bypass 4.6단계 entirely
193:   languages: [ko, en, zh, ja] # zh/ja use deterministic character-token fallback
194:   # Optional private local structural classifier. Public releases ship the
195:   # loader and feature extractor only; trained model files stay outside git/npm.
196:   # You can also set PATINA_STRUCTURAL_MODEL=/path/to/model-ko.json.
197:   # structural_model:
198:   #   path: .patina/models/model-ko.json
199:   burstiness:
200:     bands:
201:       low: 0.30              # CV < 0.30 → low (suspect). Calibrated v3.5.1 from
202:                              # 0.25 against 300 external paragraphs (HC3 + Wikipedia).
203:       high: 0.50             # CV > 0.50 → high
204:   ttr:
205:     window: 50               # MATTR window in tokens
206:     bands:
207:       low: 0.55              # MATTR < 0.55 → low (suspect)
208:       high: 0.70             # MATTR > 0.70 → high
209:   sentence_zoom:
210:     similarity_threshold: 0.20  # adjacent sentences within ±20% token count → grouped as sub-flag
211:   ko_diagnostics:
212:     enabled: true              # conservative ko-only composite; requires all bands below
213:     bands:
214:       minSentences: 4
215:       minEojeols: 20
216:       spacing:
217:         maxEojeolLengthCV: 0.38
218:       comma:
219:         maxPerSentence: 1
220:       posProxy:
221:         minMatchedCount: 10
222:         maxClassDiversity: 0.26
223:     spacing:
224:       metric: eojeol_length_cv # dependency-free Korean spacing regularity proxy
225:     comma:
226:       metric: per_sentence_and_per_100_chars
227:     pos_proxy:
228:       metric: suffix_class_diversity
229:       dependency: none         # no runtime morphology analyzer
230:   skip:
231:     min_paragraphs: 2
232:     min_sentences: 2
233: 
234: # AI-Lexicon Overlap (SKILL.md Step 4.7)
235: # Matches paragraphs against an AI-favored phrase dictionary not covered
236: # by the 28-pattern catalog. Computes density per 1000 tokens; high
237: # density paragraphs are flagged SUSPECT under the 3-signal OR rule
238: # (burstiness OR MATTR OR lexicon_density).
239: # See core/stylometry.md §16 for the full algorithm.
240: lexicon:
241:   enabled: true
242:   languages: [en, ko, zh, ja]
243:   density_threshold: 3.0     # matches per 1000 tokens; > threshold → SUSPECT
244:   # Lexicon files auto-discovered via Glob lexicon/ai-{lang}.md.
245:   # en/ko use the calibrated baseline from HC3 + Wikipedia + NamuWiki:
246:   # AI catch 66% → 76% with Wikipedia FP staying at 25% boundary.
247:   # zh/ja are starter, local-gate lexicons; expand only after a larger corpus pass.
248:   skip:
249:     min_paragraphs: 2
250:     min_sentences: 2
````
